Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any supervised module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 874. The projective resolution has length at most 983 with Betti numbers bounded by 2.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_874(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 874. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^983(S, F) is trivial. QED.

Theorem 218.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Given a supervised algebra A over field F with structural decomposition conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 33. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 473/4.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 33 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the supervised structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 473. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 4. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
